>       const igAccountId = me.user_id ?? account.platform_user_id;
>       // video-repurpose Fatia 3 — a SET of images → a native CAROUSEL post (media_type=CAROUSEL): N child image

>       const carouselImages = Array.isArray(content.images)
>         ? (content.images as unknown[]).filter((u): u is string => typeof u === "string").slice(0, 10)
>         : [];
>       if (carouselImages.length >= 2) {

>           });
>           const ch = await chRes.json();
>           if (!chRes.ok || !ch.id) { console.error("IG carousel child error:", chRes.status, ch); throw new Error("PLATFORM_PUBLISH_FAILED"); }
>           childIds.push(ch.id);
>         }
>         const parentRes = await fetch(`${IG_BASE}/${igAccountId}/media`, {
>           method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
>           body: JSON.stringify({ media_type: "CAROUSEL", children: childIds.join(","), caption: content.text || "", access_token: account.access_token }),
>         });
>         const parent = await parentRes.json();
>         if (!parentRes.ok || !parent.id) { console.error("IG carousel parent error:", parentRes.status, parent); throw new Error("PLATFORM_PUBLISH_FAILED"); }
>         const pubRes = await fetch(`${IG_BASE}/${igAccountId}/media_publish`, {
>           method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
>           body: JSON.stringify({ creation_id: parent.id, access_token: account.access_token }),
>         });
>         result = await pubRes.json();
>         if (!pubRes.ok) { console.error("Instagram carousel publish error:", pubRes.status, result); throw new Error("PLATFORM_PUBLISH_FAILED"); }
>       } else {
>       const videoUrl = content.video_url;
>       // FR-SPACES-083 (Amendment 24) — IG STORIES: same container→publish flow, media_type=STORIES; the surface
>       // discriminator rides content.surface (copied server-side by auto-publish from the reshape row, never a
>       // trusted asset ref). Stories carry no caption (the API ignores it). Covered by the SAME scope
>       // (instagram_business_content_publish) — reach stays gated on the Meta app audit like the whole IG rail.
>       // catalog (format-specs) says "story"; seed/channel naming elsewhere says "stories" — accept both.

>       const createRes = await fetch(

>           method: "POST",
>           headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
>           body: JSON.stringify(
>             isStory
>               ? { media_type: "STORIES", ...(videoUrl ? { video_url: videoUrl } : { image_url: content.image_url }), access_token: account.access_token }
>               : videoUrl
>                 ? { media_type: "REELS", video_url: videoUrl, caption: content.text || content, access_token: account.access_token }
>                 : { caption: content.text || content, image_url: content.image_url, access_token: account.access_token },
>           ),
>         }
>       );

> 
>       // REELS transcode is async — poll the container until FINISHED before publishing (≤ ~90s; short clips finish fast).

> 
>       const pubRes = await fetch(

>           method: "POST",

>       }
> 
>     } else if (platform === "facebook") {

> import { createClient } from "https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2";

> };
> 
> Deno.serve(async (req) => {
>   if (req.method === "OPTIONS") {
>     return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });
>   }
> 
>   try {
>     const adminClient = createClient(
>       Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!,
>       (Deno.env.get("SB_SECRET_KEY") ?? Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"))!
>     );
> 
>     // Find all queued posts where scheduled_at <= now
>     const { data: duePosts, error } = await adminClient
>       .from("scheduled_posts")
>       .select("*")
>       .eq("status", "queued")
>       .lte("scheduled_at", new Date().toISOString())
>       .order("scheduled_at")
>       .limit(50);
> 
>     if (error) {
>       console.error("Error fetching due posts:", error);
>       return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: error.message }), {
>         status: 500,
>         headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
>       });
>     }
> 
>     if (!duePosts || duePosts.length === 0) {
>       return new Response(JSON.stringify({ processed: 0, message: "No posts due" }), {
>         headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
>       });
>     }
> 
>     const results: Array<{ id: string; status: string; error?: string }> = [];
> 
>     for (const post of duePosts) {
>       try {
>         // Mark as publishing
>         await adminClient
>           .from("scheduled_posts")
>           .update({ status: "publishing" })
>           .eq("id", post.id);
> 
>         // Get the content body if content_id exists
>         let contentText = "";
>         if (post.content_id) {
>           const { data: content } = await adminClient
>             .from("content_library")
>             .select("body, title")
>             .eq("id", post.content_id)
>             .single();
>           contentText = content?.body || content?.title || "";
>         }
> 
>         if (!contentText) {
>           contentText = `Post agendado para ${post.platform}`;
>         }
> 
>         // ── FR-PE-011: video transport gap. The scheduled post's content_id points at the TEXT row; the 9:16 render
>         // (video-bridge.ts) lands as a SEPARATE content_library row (type='video', media_url = a path inside the
>         // private `video-studio-assets` bucket — metadata.storage_bucket) sharing this post's campaign_id. Resolve
>         // that sibling and sign it to a LONG-TTL URL so the URL survives Pinterest/IG's async fetch (a 1h signed URL
>         // dies before the publish window). Pass it as content.video_url → publish-social's REELS/TikTok/YouTube/Pin
>         // video branches. Text-only posts keep the plain-string path UNCHANGED (no campaign_id / no video sibling).
>         let videoUrl: string | null = null;
>         if (post.campaign_id) {
>           try {
>             const { data: vid } = await adminClient
>               .from("content_library")
>               .select("media_url, metadata")
>               .eq("campaign_id", post.campaign_id)
>               .eq("type", "video")
>               .eq("status", "approved")
>               .order("created_at", { ascending: false })
>               .limit(1)
>               .maybeSingle();
>             const storageKey = vid?.media_url as string | undefined;
>             if (storageKey) {
>               const bucket = (vid?.metadata as { storage_bucket?: string } | null)?.storage_bucket || "video-studio-assets";
>               if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(storageKey)) {
>                 videoUrl = storageKey; // already a public URL — pass through
>               } else {
>                 // 6h TTL — outlives the async pull (cover/video fetch) on Pinterest/IG. Private bucket → re-sign on read.
>                 const { data: signed } = await adminClient.storage
>                   .from(bucket)
>                   .createSignedUrl(storageKey, 21600);
>                 videoUrl = signed?.signedUrl ?? null;
>               }
>             }
>           } catch (e) {
>             console.warn(`[auto-publish] video_url resolve skipped for post ${post.id}:`, e instanceof Error ? e.message : e);
>           }
>         }
> 
>         // ── FR-CP-003 — prefer the reshaper's NATIVE variant over the generic derivation. ──
>         // SECURITY (FMEA-011): the asset ref is NEVER trusted from scheduled_posts.metadata (a user can write their
>         // OWN scheduled_posts.metadata, so signing a bucket/key from it with the service key would read ANY tenant's
>         // private object). Instead resolve it from the server-trusted channel_variants row, filtered by the post's
>         // OWNER (post.user_id) — that row is written service-role-only by the reshaper under the correct user_id.
>         // Signing the per-row asset key HERE (fresh 6h URL) also fixes auto-publish's single-video-per-campaign

>           // Publication privacy discriminator (plain string: 'private' | 'unlisted' | 'public'). The SAME

>               .from("creative_assets")

>               .eq("source_job_id", reshape.carousel_render_id)

> -- viral-autopilot FR-VA-005 ("cadência recorrente"): schedule the Viral Autopilot cadence driver.

> -- SOP (Lei 2): docs/processes/autopilot-cron-identity.md (§ "Topologia" + § Sequence step 1).
> --
> -- Every 15 min, pg_cron POSTs to the `autopilot-cadence-cron` edge function (service-role), which scans ACTIVE
> -- plans whose next_run_at has passed and fans out to `autopilot-run` — the atomic cap-check + single pre-debit
> -- (begin_autopilot_cycle) + prepaid fan-out + idempotent refund (finalize_autopilot_cycle) live there (single
> -- source of truth; not duplicated). A tick with no due plan is a cheap no-op (due=0), so this is safe to keep
> -- scheduled before any recurring (every_n_days) plan is ever activated — it only spends when a tenant's OWN
> -- active plan comes due, gated by that plan's MANDATORY budget/daily caps.
> --
> -- Pattern mirrors `nurture-advance` (20260603230000) + the `mcorch_embed_on_insert` Vault-key read verbatim:
> -- the Bearer is the `sb_secret_key` resolved from Vault at run time (never hardcoded; rotates with the key).
> -- `autopilot-cadence-cron` self-checks `Authorization === Bearer <SB_SECRET_KEY>` (OTD-VA-008), so ONLY this job
> -- can run it. pg_cron + pg_net are already installed (verified: nurture-advance + mcorch-stability-decay live).
> -- Idempotent: unschedule-if-exists then (re)schedule.
> --
> -- Cron path identity proven in production (Lei 1): POST without Bearer → HTTP 403 {"error":"Forbidden"};
> -- first paid cycle 947ef8b5 ran completed (spend 22 / refund 2 / balance delta -20 exact) via this exact function.
> 
> DO $$
> BEGIN
>   PERFORM cron.unschedule('autopilot-cadence');
> EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
>   NULL;  -- job did not exist yet (first apply / fresh env)
> END $$;
> 
> SELECT cron.schedule(
>   'autopilot-cadence',
>   '*/15 * * * *',
>   $cron$
>   SELECT net.http_post(
>     url := 'https://bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa.supabase.co/functions/v1/autopilot-cadence-cron',
>     headers := jsonb_build_object(
>       'Content-Type', 'application/json',
>       'Authorization', 'Bearer ' || (SELECT decrypted_secret FROM vault.decrypted_secrets WHERE name = 'sb_secret_key' LIMIT 1)
>     ),
>     body := '{}'::jsonb
>   );
>   $cron$
> );

> //   Owner-scoped (auth.uid()). Creating a plan costs 0 mco (the node does not generate — SDD §4;
> //   the G7 quote==charge invariant belongs to the CYCLE begin/finalize, never to the arm).
> //
> // IDENTITY (SDD §5.3): user-facing endpoint. This function is registered verify_jwt=false in config.toml
> //   (the sovereign-orchestration bypass block), so identity is verified IN-FUNCTION via the Supabase auth
> //   server (supabase.auth.getUser with an anon-key client bound to the caller's Authorization header — the
> //   SAME living pattern as canvas-execute:184-211 / autopilot-run:78-83). getUser() validates the JWT
> //   signature server-side (it is NOT a blind atob of the token). user_id is server-trusted from the verified
> //   claim, NEVER from the request body. Cron callers (Bearer SB_SECRET_KEY) are rejected 403 — this endpoint
> //   is arm-only (the drain/dispatch live in cadence-run, invoked by the tick).

> //   The live autopilot-cadence-cron tick routes due plans by plan_kind → cadence-run (FR-CAD-004).
> 
> import { createClient } from "https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2";
> 
> const corsHeaders = {

>   "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "POST, OPTIONS",

> 
> const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-fA-F-]{36}$/;
> const DEFAULT_TZ = "America/Sao_Paulo";        // final fallback of the fuso cascade (FR-CAD-007)
> const FREQUENCIES = new Set(["daily", "weekly", "monthly"]);
> // Fatia 1 (OTD-CAD-011): X/Twitter is OUT of the cadence allowlist until the pay-per-use probe closes
> // (Post-with-link = 45 mco, a Sovereign-gated Console action). Reject it at the edge with a 422.
> const X_CHANNELS = new Set(["x", "twitter"]);
> 
> // ── UTC re-arm calculator (FR-CAD-007 / FM-CAD-06) ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> // The cron runs in GMT and NEVER carries a time-of-day preference: the subject's wall-clock time in its IANA
> // timezone is converted to a UTC instant and stored in next_run_at. Deno has no tz library, so DST-correct
> // conversion is done with Intl.DateTimeFormat offsets (the well-known zoned-time → UTC algorithm).
> 
> // Offset (ms) such that local = utc + offset, at a given instant, for a timezone.
> function tzOffsetMs(instant: Date, tz: string): number {
>   const dtf = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
>     timeZone: tz, hourCycle: "h23",
>     year: "numeric", month: "2-digit", day: "2-digit",
>     hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", second: "2-digit",
>   });
>   const p: Record<string, string> = {};
>   for (const { type, value } of dtf.formatToParts(instant)) p[type] = value;
>   const asUTC = Date.UTC(+p.year, +p.month - 1, +p.day, +p.hour, +p.minute, +p.second);
>   return asUTC - instant.getTime();
> }
> 
> // A wall-clock (y,mo,d,h,mi) in `tz` → the UTC instant it denotes (refined once across DST boundaries).
> function zonedWallToUtc(y: number, mo: number, d: number, h: number, mi: number, tz: string): Date {
>   const wallAsUtc = Date.UTC(y, mo, d, h, mi, 0);
>   const off = tzOffsetMs(new Date(wallAsUtc), tz);
>   let ts = wallAsUtc - off;
>   const off2 = tzOffsetMs(new Date(ts), tz);
>   if (off2 !== off) ts = wallAsUtc - off2;   // DST fold/gap refinement
>   return new Date(ts);
> }
> 
> // The caller's local calendar date "today" in `tz`.
> function localYmd(instant: Date, tz: string): { y: number; mo: number; d: number } {
>   const dtf = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
>     timeZone: tz, year: "numeric", month: "2-digit", day: "2-digit",
>   });
>   const p: Record<string, string> = {};
>   for (const { type, value } of dtf.formatToParts(instant)) p[type] = value;
>   return { y: +p.year, mo: +p.month - 1, d: +p.day };
> }
> 
> // ISO weekday 1=Mon..7=Sun for a calendar date (tz-independent given Y-M-D).
> function isoDow(y: number, mo: number, d: number): number {
>   const js = new Date(Date.UTC(y, mo, d)).getUTCDay();   // 0=Sun..6=Sat
>   return js === 0 ? 7 : js;
> }
> 
> interface Recurrence {
>   frequency: "daily" | "weekly" | "monthly";
>   days: number[];        // weekly: ISO weekdays 1=Mon..7=Sun · monthly: days-of-month 1..31 · daily: ignored
>   hours: number;         // 0..23
>   minutes: number;       // 0..59, multiple of 5 (Knock semantics)
>   tz: string;            // IANA
> }
> 
> // Next occurrence strictly after `from`, as a UTC Date. Scans ≤ 430 calendar days (covers monthly day-31).
> function computeNextRunAt(rec: Recurrence, from: Date): Date | null {
>   const { y, mo, d } = localYmd(from, rec.tz);
>   const base = Date.UTC(y, mo, d);   // local "today" midnight as a pure-calendar anchor
>   for (let i = 0; i <= 430; i++) {
>     const cand = new Date(base + i * 86_400_000);
>     const cy = cand.getUTCFullYear(), cmo = cand.getUTCMonth(), cd = cand.getUTCDate();
>     let match = false;
>     if (rec.frequency === "daily") match = true;
>     else if (rec.frequency === "weekly") match = rec.days.includes(isoDow(cy, cmo, cd));
>     else if (rec.frequency === "monthly") match = rec.days.includes(cd);
>     if (!match) continue;
>     const utc = zonedWallToUtc(cy, cmo, cd, rec.hours, rec.minutes, rec.tz);
>     if (utc.getTime() > from.getTime()) return utc;
>   }
>   return null;
> }
> 
> function isValidTz(tz: unknown): tz is string {
>   if (typeof tz !== "string" || !tz) return false;
>   try { new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", { timeZone: tz }); return true; } catch { return false; }
> }
> 
> Deno.serve(async (req) => {
>   if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });
>   // Structured errors (PT-BR message for the UI toast; English code/vars per code standards).
>   const json = (b: unknown, s = 200) =>
>     new Response(JSON.stringify(b), { status: s, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } });
>   const fail422 = (field: string, message: string) => json({ error: "validation_failed", field, message }, 422);
> 
>   const url = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!;
>   const serviceKey = (Deno.env.get("SB_SECRET_KEY") ?? Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"))!;
>   const pubKey = (Deno.env.get("SB_PUBLISHABLE_KEY") ?? Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_ANON_KEY"))!;
>   const admin = createClient(url, serviceKey);
>   const logHealth = (status: string, event?: string, metadata?: Record<string, unknown>) =>
>     admin.from("infra_health_logs")
>       .insert({ service: "spaces-cadence", status, ...(event ? { event } : {}), ...(metadata ? { metadata } : {}) })
>       .then(() => {}, () => {});
> 
>   try {
>     // ── Identity (SDD §5.3): user JWT only. Cron key is rejected — arm is user-facing. ──────────────────────
>     const authHeader = req.headers.get("Authorization");
>     if (!authHeader?.startsWith("Bearer ")) return json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, 401);
>     if (authHeader === `Bearer ${serviceKey}`) return json({ error: "Forbidden" }, 403); // cron path is cadence-run
> 
>     const userClient = createClient(url, pubKey, { global: { headers: { Authorization: authHeader } } });
>     const { data: { user }, error: authErr } = await userClient.auth.getUser();
>     if (authErr || !user) return json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, 401);
>     const userId = user.id;   // server-trusted from the verified claim, never from the body
> 
>     const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
> 
>     // ── planId (optional): present ⇒ UPDATE (re-arm) an owner-scoped cadence plan; absent ⇒ INSERT a new one. ─
>     const planId = typeof body?.planId === "string" ? body.planId : null;
>     if (planId && !UUID_RE.test(planId)) return fail422("planId", "Identificador de plano inválido.");
> 
>     // ── sourceAssetId: cadence subject (creative_assets.id, owner-scoped, existing asset ⇒ 0 mco keyless). ───
>     const sourceAssetId = typeof body?.sourceAssetId === "string" ? body.sourceAssetId : null;
> ```

>   // offset(ms) = (what that wall time reads as if it were UTC) - (the same instant rendered in tz)
>   const asIfUtc = partsInTz(new Date(naiveUtc), tz);
>   const rendered = Date.UTC(asIfUtc.y, asIfUtc.mo - 1, asIfUtc.d, asIfUtc.h, asIfUtc.mi, 0);
>   const offset = rendered - naiveUtc;
>   return new Date(naiveUtc - offset);
> }
> interface Recurrence { frequency?: string; days?: number[]; hours?: number; minutes?: number; tz?: string }
> // Next occurrence strictly AFTER `after`, at hours:minutes in tz, on an allowed day. Returns UTC Date or null.
> function computeNextRunAtUtc(rec: Recurrence, tz: string, after: Date): Date | null {
>   const freq = rec.frequency ?? "daily";
>   const hours = Math.min(23, Math.max(0, Number(rec.hours ?? 9)));
>   const minutes = Math.min(59, Math.max(0, Number(rec.minutes ?? 0)));
>   const days = Array.isArray(rec.days) ? rec.days.map(Number).filter((n) => Number.isFinite(n)) : [];
>   const startLocal = partsInTz(after, tz);
>   // Walk up to ~14 months of candidate calendar days; the first whose UTC instant > after and whose day matches.
>   for (let i = 0; i < 400; i++) {
>     const cand = new Date(Date.UTC(startLocal.y, startLocal.mo - 1, startLocal.d + i, 12, 0, 0));
>     const cp = partsInTz(cand, tz);
>     let match = false;
>     if (freq === "daily") match = true;
>     else if (freq === "weekly") match = days.length === 0 || days.includes(cp.isoDow);
>     else if (freq === "monthly") match = days.length === 0 || days.includes(cp.d);
>     if (!match) continue;
>     const utc = zonedToUtc(cp.y, cp.mo, cp.d, hours, minutes, tz);
>     if (utc.getTime() > after.getTime()) return utc;
>   }
>   return null;
> }
> // Is `now` inside the plan's quiet window (subject tz)? quiet_hours = {start:'HH:MM', end:'HH:MM', days:int[]}.
> function inQuietHours(quiet: unknown, tz: string, now: Date): boolean {
>   const q = (quiet ?? null) as { start?: string; end?: string; days?: number[] } | null;
>   if (!q?.start || !q?.end) return false;
>   const np = partsInTz(now, tz);
>   const qDays = Array.isArray(q.days) ? q.days : [];
>   if (qDays.length > 0 && !qDays.includes(np.isoDow)) return false;
>   const toMin = (s: string) => { const [h, m] = s.split(":").map(Number); return (h || 0) * 60 + (m || 0); };
>   const nowMin = np.h * 60 + np.mi;
>   const s = toMin(q.start), e = toMin(q.end);
>   return s <= e ? (nowMin >= s && nowMin < e) : (nowMin >= s || nowMin < e); // wrap past midnight
> }
> async function sha256Hex(input: string): Promise<string> {
>   const buf = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(input));
>   return Array.from(new Uint8Array(buf)).map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
> }
> 
> Deno.serve(async (req) => {
>   if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });
>   const json = (b: unknown, s = 200) =>
>     new Response(JSON.stringify(b), { status: s, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } });
> 
>   const url = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!;
>   const serviceKey = (Deno.env.get("SB_SECRET_KEY") ?? Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"))!;
>   const admin = createClient(url, serviceKey);
>   // Telemetry chokepoint (NFR-CAD-015). `event`/`metadata` allowlisted — never leak per-tenant PII into the
> ```

>         try {
>           // Tenant override first (lowest priority number), else the system seed row (user_id IS NULL).
>           const { data: profs } = await admin.from("channel_profiles")
>             .select("image, user_id, priority")
>             .eq("channel", channel).eq("surface", surface).eq("is_active", true)
>             .or(`user_id.is.null,user_id.eq.${user.id}`)
>             .order("priority", { ascending: true }).limit(5);
>           const spec = (profs ?? []).map((p) => p.image as { width?: number; height?: number } | null)
>             .find((im) => Number(im?.width) > 0 && Number(im?.height) > 0);
>           const tw = Number(spec?.width) || 0;
>           const th = Number(spec?.height) || 0;
>           if (!tw || !th) {
>             reframeSkipReason = "no_image_spec_for_surface";
>           } else if (tw > 2048 || th > 2048) {
>             reframeSkipReason = "target_too_large";
>           } else if (Number(asset.width) > 0 && Number(asset.height) > 0 && Number(asset.width) * Number(asset.height) > 6_000_000) {
>             // Source-DIMENSION guard (uncatchable WORKER_RESOURCE_LIMIT prevention, witnessed 2026-07-17 on
>             // roadmap-master 2480×8474 = 21MP in a ~1MB PNG): imagescript decodes the FULL source in-process, so a
>             // highly-compressed but huge image passes the ≤15MB file cap yet OOMs the worker on decode — and the
>             // runtime kills the worker BEFORE the try/catch can fail-open. Skip reframe above ~6MP → publish-as-is.
>             reframeSkipReason = "source_dimensions_too_large";
>           } else {
>             const { data: blob } = await admin.storage.from(publishBucket).download(publishKey);
>             if (!blob) throw new Error("source_download_failed");
>             if (blob.size > 15 * 1024 * 1024) {
>               reframeSkipReason = "source_too_large";
>             } else {
>               const jpeg = await reframeToJpeg(new Uint8Array(await blob.arrayBuffer()), tw, th);
>               // channel/surface are client strings — slug-sanitize before they touch a storage PATH (service-role
>               // writes bypass storage policies, so a '../' here could escape the owner prefix; DB usage is
>               // parameterized and safe, the PATH is the boundary). The short hash disambiguates distinct
>               // (channel,surface,target) tuples whose slugs collide (e.g. "a.b" vs "a_b") — same-tenant only,
>               // but a collision would silently overwrite the earlier derived JPEG.
>               const slug = (s: string) => s.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_]/g, "_").slice(0, 40);
>               const rawTuple = new TextEncoder().encode(`${channel}|${surface}|${tw}x${th}`);
>               const digest = new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", rawTuple));
>               const tupleHash = Array.from(digest.slice(0, 4)).map((x) => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
>               const outKey = `${user.id}/publish-reframe/${asset.id}-${slug(channel)}-${slug(surface)}-${tupleHash}.jpg`;
>               const { error: upRfErr } = await admin.storage.from("canvas-assets")
>                 .upload(outKey, jpeg, { contentType: "image/jpeg", upsert: true });
>               if (upRfErr) throw new Error(upRfErr.message);
>               // Lineage row — select-first keeps re-calls idempotent. creative_assets HAS a UNIQUE on
>               // (storage_bucket, storage_key) (creative_assets_uq_object), so a concurrent duplicate INSERT
>               // fails 23505 → warn below → publish still proceeds (equivalent, never an overwrite).
>               const { data: existing } = await admin.from("creative_assets")
>                 .select("id").eq("user_id", user.id).eq("storage_bucket", "canvas-assets").eq("storage_key", outKey).maybeSingle();
>               if (!existing) {
>                 const { error: insErr } = await admin.from("creative_assets").insert({
>                   user_id: user.id, kind: "image", storage_bucket: "canvas-assets", storage_key: outKey,
>                   mime_type: "image/jpeg", file_size_bytes: jpeg.byteLength, width: tw, height: th,
>                   parent_asset_id: asset.id, source_module: "spaces",
>                   title: `reframe ${channel}/${surface}`,
>                   metadata: { reframe: { channel, surface, target: { w: tw, h: th } } },
>                 });
>                 if (insErr) console.warn("[publish-space-asset] derived lineage insert failed (asset still published):", insErr.message);
>               }
>               publishBucket = "canvas-assets";
>               publishKey = outKey;
>               reframed = true;
>             }
>           }
>         } catch (re) {
>           reframeSkipReason = re instanceof Error ? re.message : String(re);
>           console.warn(`[publish-space-asset] reframe fail-open (${channel}/${surface}):`, reframeSkipReason);
>         }
>       }
>     }
> 
>     // ── Upsert the variant (idempotent on user_id, source_asset_id, channel, surface). status/scheduled_post_id are
>     //    NOT in the payload → on conflict they are NOT downgraded (a re-call never un-schedules a scheduled variant). ──
>     const { data: variant, error: upErr } = await admin.from("space_publish_variants")
>       .upsert({
>         user_id: user.id,
>         space_id: spaceId,
>         node_run_id: nodeRunId,
>         source_asset_id: asset.id,
>         channel,
>         surface,
>         platform,
>         native_text: nativeText,
>         asset_kind: assetKind,
>         asset_bucket: publishBucket,   // SERVER-TRUSTED (owner row, or the server-derived reframe of it) — never from the client
>         asset_key: publishKey,
>         asset_status: "ready",
>       }, { onConflict: "user_id,source_asset_id,channel,surface" })
>       .select("id, status, scheduled_post_id, platform").single();
>     if (upErr || !variant) return json(500, { error: "upsert_failed", detail: upErr?.message });
> 
>     // ── Draft mode (default): variant created/refreshed, publishable later. ──
>     if (!wantPublish) {
>       return json(200, {
>         ok: true, space_publish_variant_id: variant.id, status: variant.status, platform: variant.platform,
>         reframed, ...(reframeSkipReason ? { reframe_skip_reason: reframeSkipReason } : {}),
>       });
>     }
> 
>     // ── Publish mode: enqueue into scheduled_posts (auto-publish resolves the asset owner-scoped from the variant). ──
>     if (!variant.platform) {
>       return json(422, { error: "surface_not_publishable", action: "Este canal/superfície não mapeia para uma rede publicável." });
>     }
>     if (variant.scheduled_post_id || variant.status === "scheduled" || variant.status === "published") {
>       // Return the variant status so the client can distinguish "still in flight" from "already
>       // published" — the toast/label differs, the recovery differs (cancel vs. cannot cancel).
>       return json(409, {
>         error: "already_enqueued",
>         scheduled_post_id: variant.scheduled_post_id,
>         status: variant.status,
>       });
>     }
> 
>     const text = typeof nativeText.caption === "string" ? nativeText.caption
>       : typeof nativeText.body === "string" ? nativeText.body
>       : typeof nativeText.title === "string" ? nativeText.title : "";
>     // Sovereign default — fail-SAFE, never fail-open: a YouTube publish with nothing wired from the UI goes out
>     // PRIVATE. The last reversible step is taken autonomously; flipping it public stays a Sovereign act. This
>     // makes a public post-by-forgotten-wiring structurally impossible. Other platforms keep the platform default.
>     const privacy = requestedPrivacy ?? (variant.platform === "youtube" ? "private" : null);
>     const content: Record<string, unknown> = { text, surface, channel };
>     if (privacy) content.privacy = privacy;
>     if (Array.isArray(nativeText.tweets)) content.tweets = nativeText.tweets;
>     // FR-VR — destination link (the full-episode CTA, UTM-tagged). Rides content.reshape → auto-publish forwards
>     // it to publish-social; Pinterest turns it into the native clickable pin (the one funnel with a real click).
>     if (typeof body.link === "string" && /^https?:\/\//.test(body.link)) content.link = body.link.slice(0, 2048);
> 
>     const { data: sp, error: spErr } = await admin.from("scheduled_posts").insert({
>       user_id: user.id, content_id: null, campaign_id: null,
>       social_account_id: typeof body.social_account_id === "string" ? body.social_account_id : null,
>       platform: variant.platform, scheduled_at: publishAt ?? new Date().toISOString(), status: "queued",
>       metadata: { reshape: { content, space_publish_variant_id: variant.id, surface, channel } },
>     }).select("id").single();
>     if (spErr || !sp) return json(500, { error: "enqueue_failed", detail: spErr?.message });
> 
>     await admin.from("space_publish_variants")
>       .update({ status: "scheduled", scheduled_post_id: sp.id }).eq("id", variant.id);
> 
>     // Mesh Connection Mandate + telemetry (best-effort).
>     admin.from("infra_health_logs").insert({ service: "publish-space-asset", status: "healthy" }).then(() => {}, () => {});
> 
>     return json(200, {
>       ok: true, space_publish_variant_id: variant.id, scheduled_post_id: sp.id,
>       status: "scheduled", platform: variant.platform,
>       reframed, ...(reframeSkipReason ? { reframe_skip_reason: reframeSkipReason } : {}),
>     });
>   } catch (e) {
>     console.error("publish-space-asset error:", e);
>     return json(500, { error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Unknown error" });
>   }
> });
> ```

## 🤖 Claude *(02:29:28)*

<details>
<summary>💭 Thinking Process</summary>

The shortest approach is using `publish-space-asset` with a future `publish_at` timestamp, which creates a variant and enqueues a scheduled post that auto-publishes at that time. Since the variant is idempotent on the combination of user, source asset, channel, and surface, each asset can only have one scheduled post per channel-surface pair—so for multiple days with distinct assets, I'd make one call per asset per channel. Now I'm checking how the UI handles this...…

</details>

> ```
> #!/usr/bin/env bun
> /**
>  * scripts/youtube-upload-bridge.ts
>  *
>  * YouTube Track B "Fábrica" — host worker that polls `youtube_uploads` (state='queued'), atomically claims each
>  * (begin_youtube_upload), and runs a RESUMABLE `videos.insert` streaming the host master from repurpose-inbox/<uid>/
>  * in 8 MB chunks (memory-bounded — a 1.3 GB master never loads whole), then closes the job via
>  * finalize_youtube_upload (the ONLY terminal authority). Optional force-ssl extras (thumbnail/caption/playlist)
>  * run fail-soft after the insert (the video already exists).
>  *
>  * BoK SSOT: docs/bok/youtube-studio/13-amendment-upload-factory.md (FR-YT-026/027/028) — molde scripts/video-bridge.ts.
>  * WHY a host worker (not an edge fn): an Edge Function cannot stream 1.3 GB (memory/timeout) and cannot reach the
>  * host inbox where the master lives (host-upload-server.ts). Token resolved server-side from decrypted_social_accounts
>  * (API Tenancy Model), never from the client. privacyStatus defaults to 'private' — never auto-public.
>  *
>  * Supervisor: systemd --user youtube-upload.service (Restart=always). Secrets load from ../.env (NOT in the unit).
>  * Usage: bun run scripts/youtube-upload-bridge.ts   (or `--once` to drain queued uploads and exit — used by the smoke).
>  */
> 
> import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
> import { existsSync, readFileSync, statSync, openSync, readSync, closeSync, mkdtempSync, rmSync, createWriteStream } from 'fs';
> import { join, dirname, resolve as pathResolve } from 'path';
> import { fileURLToPath } from 'url';
> import { tmpdir } from 'os';
> import { Readable } from 'stream';
> import { pipeline } from 'stream/promises';
> 
> // ── Env (same loader as video-bridge.ts — no secrets in the systemd unit) ──
> const __dirname = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
> const envPath = join(__dirname, '../.env');
> if (existsSync(envPath)) {
>   for (const line of readFileSync(envPath, 'utf-8').split('\n')) {
>     const m = line.match(/^([^#=\s][^=]*)=(.*)$/);
>     if (m) process.env[m[1].trim()] = m[2].trim().replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '');
>   }
> }
> 
> const SUPABASE_URL = process.env.SUPABASE_URL;
> const SERVICE_KEY = process.env.SB_SECRET_KEY ?? process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY;
> if (!SUPABASE_URL || !SERVICE_KEY) {
>   console.error('❌ Missing SUPABASE_URL or SB_SECRET_KEY in .env');
>   process.exit(1);
> }
> const supabase = createClient(SUPABASE_URL, SERVICE_KEY, { auth: { persistSession: false } });
> 
> const INBOX_BASE = pathResolve(__dirname, '../repurpose-inbox');
> const POLL_MS = 5000;
> const RUNNING_TIMEOUT_MS = 60 * 60 * 1000;            // re-claim an upload stuck 'running' beyond 60 min (dead worker)
> const CHUNK = 8 * 1024 * 1024;                        // 8 MB — multiple of 256 KB (YouTube resumable requirement)
> const SCOPE_UPLOAD = 'https://www.googleapis.com/auth/youtube';        // videos.insert
> const SCOPE_FORCE_SSL = 'https://www.googleapis.com/auth/youtube.force-ssl'; // thumbnail/caption
> const UPLOAD_ENDPOINT = 'https://www.googleapis.com/upload/youtube/v3/videos';
> const YT_DATA = 'https://www.googleapis.com/youtube/v3';
> 
> type Json = Record<string, unknown>;
> // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
> type Any = any; // Google API resource shapes — untyped by design (host worker, not app-typed)
> 
> function logHealth(status: string, event: string, metadata?: Json) {
>   return supabase.from('infra_health_logs')
>     .insert({ service: 'youtube-upload', status, event, metadata: metadata ?? null })
>     .then(() => {}, () => {});
> }
> 
> /** Resolve a usable OAuth token for the job's owner — mirrors youtube-data ensureFreshToken (audited refresh). */
> async function resolveToken(userId: string): Promise<{ token: string; scopes: string[] } | { error: string }> {
>   const { data: account, error } = await supabase
>     .from('decrypted_social_accounts')
>     .select('*')
>     .eq('user_id', userId)
>     .eq('platform', 'youtube')
>     .eq('is_active', true)
>     .maybeSingle();
>   if (error) return { error: 'account_lookup_failed' };
>   if (!account) return { error: 'youtube_not_connected' };
> 
>   const scopes: string[] = (account.scopes as string[] | null) ?? [];
>   if (!scopes.includes(SCOPE_UPLOAD)) return { error: 'youtube_scope_missing' };
> 
>   const expMs = account.token_expires_at ? new Date(account.token_expires_at).getTime() : 0;
> ```

## 🤖 Claude *(02:30:21)*

<details>
<summary>💭 Thinking Process</summary>

I'm spotting a critical issue: the Calendar's scheduling dialog only captures platform, scheduled time, content ID, and social account—but it has no field for attaching creative assets or videos. This means posts scheduled through the Calendar would reach publish-social with just text, causing failures on video-required platforms like TikTok, YouTube, Instagram Reels, and Pinterest. The problem compounds because the content ID references the content library, and auto-publish would normally find a video sibling through the campaign ID, but the Calendar sets that to null, so there's no way to resolve the video.…

</details>

>
> ```
> // Shared: OAuth APP credential resolution for the 6 social platforms.
> // API Tenancy Model (CLAUDE.md): resolve credentials per-user first, then the admin-managed GLOBAL
> // row, env only as a legacy/emergency fallback, hard-fail otherwise — never a silent shared key.
> // BoK SSOT: docs/bok/post-engine/10-amendment-social-connect-*.md (FR-PE-005..009) +
> // 16-amendment-global-app-credentials-admin-tier.md (FR-PE-017/018 — closes OTD-PE-SOCIAL-APPCREDS).
> //
> // Resolution order (UNBREAKABLE):
> //   (1) social_app_config per-user (user_id = caller, is_active) — tenant BYOK.
> //   (2) social_app_config GLOBAL  (user_id IS NULL,   is_active) — admin-managed via /dashboard/admin.
> //   (3) env fallback — legacy/emergency only (no longer the operational path).
> //   (4) SocialAppConfigError(402) {error:"<platform>_not_configured", action:"Configure ..."}.
> //
> // SECURITY: client_secret lives encrypted in Vault; only the service-role decrypted_social_app_config view
> // exposes plaintext. NEVER read the masked public view for the secret (it returns '••••••••••••').
> 
> import type { SupabaseClient } from "https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2";
> 
> export type SocialAppPlatform =
>   | "tiktok"
>   | "youtube"
>   | "pinterest"
>   | "linkedin"
>   | "instagram"
>   | "twitter";
> 
> export interface SocialAppCreds {
>   clientId: string;
>   clientSecret: string;
>   scopes: string[] | null;
>   metadata: Record<string, unknown>;
>   source: "per-user" | "global" | "env";
> }
> 
> // Values are env-var NAMES (looked up in Deno.env), NOT secret values. The `*Env` suffix keeps them
> // unambiguous to the secret-scan hook (a `secret: "..."` literal would false-positive).
> // Note: the "facebook" branch of the OAuth rails uses the instagram (Meta) app credentials.
> const ENV_MAP: Record<SocialAppPlatform, { idEnv: string; secretEnv: string; label: string }> = {
>   tiktok: { idEnv: "TIKTOK_CLIENT_KEY", secretEnv: "TIKTOK_CLIENT_SECRET", label: "TikTok" },
>   youtube: { idEnv: "GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID", secretEnv: "GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET", label: "YouTube" },
>   pinterest: { idEnv: "PINTEREST_CLIENT_ID", secretEnv: "PINTEREST_CLIENT_SECRET", label: "Pinterest" },
>   linkedin: { idEnv: "LINKEDIN_CLIENT_ID", secretEnv: "LINKEDIN_CLIENT_SECRET", label: "LinkedIn" },
>   instagram: { idEnv: "INSTAGRAM_APP_ID", secretEnv: "INSTAGRAM_APP_SECRET", label: "Instagram/Meta" },
>   twitter: { idEnv: "TWITTER_CLIENT_ID", secretEnv: "TWITTER_CLIENT_SECRET", label: "X (Twitter)" },
> };
> 
> /** Structured fail-closed error → maps to a 402 JSON body the client can act on. */
> export class SocialAppConfigError extends Error {
>   readonly httpStatus = 402;
>   readonly body: { error: string; action: string };
>   constructor(platform: string, label: string) {
>     super(`${platform}_not_configured`);
>     this.name = "SocialAppConfigError";
>     this.body = {
>       error: `${platform}_not_configured`,
>       action: `Configure suas credenciais de app ${label} em /dashboard/settings`,
>     };
>   }
> }
> 
> /**
>  * Resolve OAuth app credentials for a platform, scoped to a user.
>  * @param supabase a SERVICE-ROLE client (reads the decrypted_social_app_config view).
>  * @throws SocialAppConfigError(402) when neither per-user nor env credentials resolve.
>  */
> export async function resolveSocialAppCreds(
>   supabase: SupabaseClient,
>   userId: string,
>   platform: SocialAppPlatform,
> ): Promise<SocialAppCreds> {
>   const env = ENV_MAP[platform];
>   if (!env) throw new SocialAppConfigError(platform, platform);
> 
>   // (1) per-user (tenant BYOK)
>   if (userId) {
>     const { data, error } = await supabase
>       .from("decrypted_social_app_config")
>       .select("client_id, client_secret, scopes, metadata, is_active")
>       .eq("user_id", userId)
>       .eq("platform", platform)
>       .eq("is_active", true)
>       .maybeSingle();
> 
>     if (!error && data && data.client_id && data.client_secret) {
>       return {
>         clientId: String(data.client_id),
>         clientSecret: String(data.client_secret),
>         scopes: (data.scopes as string[] | null) ?? null,
>         metadata: (data.metadata as Record<string, unknown>) ?? {},
>         source: "per-user",
>       };
>     }
>   }
> 
>   // (2) GLOBAL row — admin-managed via /dashboard/admin (FR-PE-017/018)
>   {
>     const { data, error } = await supabase
>       .from("decrypted_social_app_config")
>       .select("client_id, client_secret, scopes, metadata, is_active")
>       .is("user_id", null)
>       .eq("platform", platform)
>       .eq("is_active", true)
>       .maybeSingle();
> 
>     if (!error && data && data.client_id && data.client_secret) {
>       return {
>         clientId: String(data.client_id),
>         clientSecret: String(data.client_secret),
>         scopes: (data.scopes as string[] | null) ?? null,
>         metadata: (data.metadata as Record<string, unknown>) ?? {},
>         source: "global",
>       };
>     }
>   }
> 
>   // (3) env fallback (legacy/emergency — the admin tier is the operational global path)
>   const envId = Deno.env.get(env.idEnv);
>   const envSecret = Deno.env.get(env.secretEnv);
>   if (envId && envSecret) {
>     return { clientId: envId, clientSecret: envSecret, scopes: null, metadata: {}, source: "env" };
>   }
> ```

## 🤖 Claude *(02:34:15)*

<details>
<summary>💭 Thinking Process</summary>

I'm noticing inconsistencies in how different parts of the codebase handle surface types—Instagram uses singular "reel" in format specs but plural "reels" in publish destinations, while the auto-publish and publish-social functions treat stories and videos differently depending on whether they check the isStory flag or just default to REELS based on the videoUrl. There's also a mismatch where Pinterest uses "pin" as the surface identifier.

Looking at the TikTok setup, the account was approved back in August 2026 and requires the creator's account to be public, but there's a critical issue: there are two active TikTok accounts in the database, and publish-social uses `.limit(1).single()` to fetch one, which means it arbitrarily picks whichever row comes first without any ordering—one token expires August 7th and the other July 31st, both already expired. This arbitrary selection problem is exactly what the social_account_id parameter was meant to solve.…

</details>

## 🤖 Claude *(02:34:36)*

<details>
<summary>💭 Thinking Process</summary>

I'm realizing that `publish-meta` isn't actually called by anything—it's a dead code path for scheduling. More importantly, I've spotted a critical bug in the duplicate logic: `handleDuplicate` copies the platform, scheduled time, content ID, and social account ID, but it skips the metadata entirely. This means duplicating a video post through Kanban or Tabela would lose the `metadata.reshape` property, causing the duplicate to publish as caption-only and fail the `*_no_video` checks. That's a nasty trap for the "duplicate for N days" workflow. Let me trace through this manually to confirm.…

</details>

## 🤖 Claude *(02:37:07)*

<details>
<summary>💭 Thinking Process</summary>

I'm cleaning up the scratchpad file and then structuring the output with platform rails as "trilho" entries and scheduler/UI capabilities as "capacidade" items.

Now I'm enumerating each platform: Instagram has a working Reels/Stories rail with a live token through 2026, 9 published items ready to post vertically; LinkedIn supports text and images only with no video capability; Pinterest has a complete video pin workflow but is gated by trial status; TikTok's token expired in August though refresh credentials are present, and its published posts lack proper IDs.

YouTube has two implementations—one edge-based with resumable uploads that's unproven, another via a worker process that handles scheduling but only shows 16:9 masters, not shorts; Facebook's token is invalid due to a mismatched IG Business Login; Twitter/X's token is also expired.

The scheduling infrastructure exists—a cron job that's been running since recovering from an API key error, a queued posts sink with proper access controls, and a publish-space-asset path for future-dated variants—but there are zero cadence plans or dispatches actually configured.

The pg_cron and pg_net jobs have been dead since August 8th, and the UI layer has fundamental gaps: the calendar can't create scheduled posts with video assets, the pipeline views lose metadata when duplicating, and the asset dialogs publish immediately rather than scheduling. The publications panel is incomplete.

YouTube uploads handle client-side RLS with native publish timing, signed URLs last six hours, channel profile caps only apply during cadence runs, and OAuth credentials are present across all platforms with viable refresh. The publish-meta table appears orphaned with no callers.

I've documented about 22 key issues, though I couldn't fully verify whether the automatic token refresh actually executes as expected. Não consegui verificar se as contas TikTok e YouTube estão públicas hoje (tokens 401), e sem um ORDER BY na query, não sei qual das duas linhas TikTok o publish-social escolhe — a ordem é arbitrária. Pinterest nunca publicou um vídeo pin; o gate de trial sandbox vem só do código, não de uma chamada à API. Também não medi o limite real de tamanho das edge functions, mas os vídeos variam de 8MB até 28MB.…

</details>
